# DELF French Verbs Master Reference (681 Verbs)
## Complete Conjugations, Pronunciation, Audio & Syntax Notes

| # | French Verb | English Meaning | Pronunciation | Audio | Verb Group | Example Sentence | Present Tense Conjugation Drill | Liaison & Teacher's Notes |
|:---|:---|:---|:---|:---:|:---|:---|:---|:---|
| 1 | **abaisser** | to lower | `abehss-SAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=abaisser&op=translate) | Verb (1st group -er) | Il abaisse la vitre de la voiture. *(He lowers the car window.)* | `j'abaisse, tu abaisses, il abaisse, nous abaissons, vous abaissez, ils abaissent` | Auxiliary: avoir | Mandatory elision with je (*j'*); [z] liaison with plural pronouns: *nous‿*, *vous‿*, *ils‿*. |
| 2 | **abasourdir** | to daze, bewilder | `abasoord-EER` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=abasourdir&op=translate) | Verb (2nd group -ir) | Elle souhaite abasourdir pour réussir son projet. *(She wishes to daze to succeed in her project.)* | `j'abasourdis, tu abasourdis, il abasourdit, nous abasourdissons, vous abasourdissez, ils abasourdissent` | Auxiliary: avoir | Mandatory elision with je (*j'*); [z] liaison with plural pronouns: *nous‿*, *vous‿*, *ils‿*. |
| 3 | **abattre** | to knock down | `abatt-truh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=abattre&op=translate) | Verb (3rd group / irregular) | La tempête abat un vieil arbre. *(The storm knocks down an old tree.)* | `j'abats, tu abats, il abat, nous abattons, vous abattez, ils abattent` | Auxiliary: avoir | Mandatory elision with je (*j'*); [z] liaison with plural pronouns: *nous‿*, *vous‿*, *ils‿*. |
| 4 | **abolir** | to abolish | `abol-EER` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=abolir&op=translate) | Verb (2nd group -ir) | Le pays décide d'abolir cette loi ancienne. *(The country decides to abolish this old law.)* | `j'abolis, tu abolis, il abolit, nous abolissons, vous abolissez, ils abolissent` | Auxiliary: avoir | Mandatory elision with je (*j'*); [z] liaison with plural pronouns: *nous‿*, *vous‿*, *ils‿*. |
| 5 | **absoudre** | to absolve | `absood-druh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=absoudre&op=translate) | Verb (3rd group / irregular) | Elle souhaite absoudre pour réussir son projet. *(She wishes to absolve to succeed in her project.)* | `j'absous, tu absous, il absout, nous absolvons, vous absolvez, ils absolvent` | Auxiliary: avoir | Mandatory elision with je (*j'*); [z] liaison with plural pronouns: *nous‿*, *vous‿*, *ils‿*. |
| 6 | **s'abstenir** | to abstain | `sahb-abstahn-NEER` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=s%27abstenir&op=translate) | Reflexive Verb (3rd group / irregular) | Il s'abstenir régulièrement pendant ses activités quotidiennes. *(He abstain regularly during his daily activities.)* | `je m'abstiens, tu t'abstiens, il s'abstient, nous nous abstenons, vous vous abstenez, ils s'abstiennent` | Auxiliary: être *(all reflexive verbs take être in compound tenses)* | Mandatory elision with je (*j'*); [z] liaison with plural pronouns: *nous‿*, *vous‿*, *ils‿*. |
